The model captures the graded stiffness profile induced by the tapering and enables systematic exploration of the configuration space as a function of the geometric design parameters. Specifically, we analyze how the backbone taper angle influences the robot's configuration space and manipulability. The model is validated against motion capture data, achieving centimeter-level shape prediction accuracy after calibrating Young's modulus via a line search that minimizes modeling error. We further demonstrate teleoperated grasping using an endoscopic gripper routed along the continuum robot, mounted on a 6-DoF robotic arm. Parameterized iLogic/CAD scripts are provided for rapid geometry generation and scaling. The presented framework establishes a simple, rapid, and reproducible pathway from parametric design to controlled tendon actuation for tapered, tendon-driven continuum robots manufactured using fused deposition modeling 3D printers.

</details>


### [188] [Introducing M: A Modular, Modifiable Social Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19134)
*Victor Nikhil Antony,Zhili Gong,Yoonjae Kim,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: M是一个开源、低成本的社会机器人平台，旨在通过简化机器人复制、修改和部署来减少社会机器人研究中的平台摩擦


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少社会机器人研究中的平台摩擦，使机器人更容易复制、修改和部署到现实世界环境中，支持纵向、可重复的社会机器人研究

Method: 结合模块化机械设计、多模态传感和表达性但机械简单的驱动架构，采用ROS2原生软件包，清晰分离感知、表达控制和数据管理，并提供与硬件接口等效的仿真环境

Result: 展示了通过附加传感/驱动模块的可扩展性，提供了讲故事和双向对话辅导的交互模板，并在参与式设计和为期一周的家庭部署中进行了实际应用验证

Conclusion: M平台可以作为纵向、可重复社会机器人研究的实用基础，通过降低平台摩擦加速社会机器人研究的发展

Abstract: We present M, an open-source, low-cost social robot platform designed to reduce platform friction that slows social robotics research by making robots easier to reproduce, modify, and deploy in real-world settings. M combines a modular mechanical design, multimodal sensing, and expressive yet mechanically simple actuation architecture with a ROS2-native software package that cleanly separates perception, expression control, and data management. The platform includes a simulation environment with interface equivalence to hardware to support rapid sim-to-real transfer of interaction behaviors. We demonstrate extensibility through additional sensing/actuation modules and provide example interaction templates for storytelling and two-way conversational coaching. Finally, we report real-world use in participatory design and week-long in-home deployments, showing how M can serve as a practical foundation for longitudinal, reproducible social robotics research.

</details>


### [189] [Meanings and Measurements: Multi-Agent Probabilistic Grounding for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19166)
*Swagat Padhan,Lakshya Jain,Bhavya Minesh Shah,Omkar Patil,Thao Nguyen,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAPG框架解决机器人理解复杂度量语义语言查询的问题，通过多智能体概率性分解和组合语言查询，在3D空间中生成度量一致的可执行决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在语义接地方面表现良好，但缺乏对物理空间中度量约束的显式推理能力，导致处理复杂度量语义语言查询时存在困难。

Method: 提出多智能体概率接地(MAPG)框架：将语言查询分解为结构化子组件，查询视觉语言模型为每个组件接地，然后概率性地组合这些接地输出以生成3D空间中度量一致的可执行决策。

Result: 在HM-EQA基准测试中相比强基线获得一致性能提升；提出新基准MAPG-Bench专门评估度量语义目标接地；真实机器人演示显示MAPG在结构化场景表示可用时可迁移到实际应用。

Conclusion: MAPG框架有效解决了复杂度量语义语言查询的接地问题，填补了现有语言接地评估的空白，并在仿真和真实机器人场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Robots collaborating with humans must convert natural language goals into actionable, physically grounded decisions. For example, executing a command such as "go two meters to the right of the fridge" requires grounding semantic references, spatial relations, and metric constraints within a 3D scene. While recent vision language models (VLMs) demonstrate strong semantic grounding capabilities, they are not explicitly designed to reason about metric constraints in physically defined spaces. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that state-of-the-art VLM-based grounding approaches struggle with complex metric-semantic language queries. To address this limitation, we propose MAPG (Multi-Agent Probabilistic Grounding), an agentic framework that decomposes language queries into structured subcomponents and queries a VLM to ground each component. MAPG then probabilistically composes these grounded outputs to produce metrically consistent, actionable decisions in 3D space. We evaluate MAPG on the HM-EQA benchmark and show consistent performance improvements over strong baselines. Furthermore, we introduce a new benchmark, MAPG-Bench, specifically designed to evaluate metric-semantic goal grounding, addressing a gap in existing language grounding evaluations. We also present a real-world robot demonstration showing that MAPG transfers beyond simulation when a structured scene representation is available.

</details>


### [190] [ADMM-Based Distributed MPC with Control Barrier Functions for Safe Multi-Robot Quadrupedal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19170)
*Yicheng Zeng,Ruturaj S. Sambhus,Basit Muhammad Imran,Jeeseop Kim,Vittorio Pastore,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于多机器人腿式系统安全关键轨迹规划的完全分散式模型预测控制框架，结合控制屏障函数约束，通过ADMM分布式优化实现并行计算，在保持安全性的同时显著减少规划时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为多机器人腿式系统设计安全关键的轨迹规划框架，需要解决控制屏障函数约束引入的智能体间耦合问题，实现完全分散式计算以避免集中式优化的通信和计算瓶颈。

Method: 采用基于交替方向乘子法的结构化分布式优化框架，提出新颖的节点-边分裂公式化方法，将全局问题分解为独立的节点局部和边局部二次规划问题，实现仅需邻居间通信的并行求解。

Result: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人硬件实验和最多四个机器人的数值仿真中验证了有效性，分布式公式化在四智能体情况下将每周期平均规划时间减少高达51%，性能与集中式MPC相当。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式框架实现了完全分散的轨迹优化，在保持安全性和动态可行性的同时，通过对称计算负载和并行处理显著提高了实时性能，适用于多机器人腿式系统的安全关键导航任务。

Abstract: This paper proposes a fully decentralized model predictive control (MPC) framework with control barrier function (CBF) constraints for safety-critical trajectory planning in multi-robot legged systems. The incorporation of CBF constraints introduces explicit inter-agent coupling, which prevents direct decomposition of the resulting optimal control problems. To address this challenge, we reformulate the centralized safety-critical MPC problem using a structured distributed optimization framework based on the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). By introducing a novel node-edge splitting formulation with consensus constraints, the proposed approach decomposes the global problem into independent node-local and edge-local quadratic programs that can be solved in parallel using only neighbor-to-neighbor communication. This enables fully decentralized trajectory optimization with symmetric computational load across agents while preserving safety and dynamic feasibility. The proposed framework is integrated into a hierarchical locomotion control architecture for quadrupedal robots, combining high-level distributed trajectory planning, mid-level nonlinear MPC enforcing single rigid body dynamics, and low-level whole-body control enforcing full-order robot dynamics. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through hardware experiments on two Unitree Go2 quadrupedal robots and numerical simulations involving up to four robots navigating uncertain environments with rough terrain and external disturbances. The results show that the proposed distributed formulation achieves performance comparable to centralized MPC while reducing the average per-cycle planning time by up to 51% in the four-agent case, enabling efficient real-time decentralized implementation.

</details>


### [191] [Sparse Autoencoders Reveal Interpretable and Steerable Features in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19183)
*Aiden Swann,Lachlain McGranahan,Hugo Buurmeijer,Monroe Kennedy,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究应用机制可解释性技术分析VLA模型，发现大多数特征对应训练演示的记忆序列，但少数特征对应可解释、通用、可操控的运动基元和语义属性，揭示了VLA泛化能力的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中表现出不一致的泛化能力：在某些场景表现优异，但在新对象、场景和指令上微调版本经常失败。需要理解VLA模型的内部工作机制，以解释其泛化不一致的原因。

Method: 应用机制可解释性技术，在VLA隐藏层激活上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），学习稀疏字典特征作为模型计算的可解释基础。提出特征分类指标区分通用可迁移基元与特定记忆。在LIBERO基准上进行操控实验验证。

Result: 发现大多数SAE特征对应特定训练演示的记忆序列，但少数特征对应可解释、通用、可操控的运动基元和语义属性。单个SAE特征能因果影响机器人行为，操控通用特征可跨任务和场景诱导一致行为。监督微调会放大记忆，而大规模多样化数据集或知识隔离促进通用特征。

Conclusion: 首次提供机制证据表明VLA能跨任务和场景学习通用特征。稀疏自编码器特征分析揭示了VLA泛化能力的内部机制，为改进模型泛化提供了方向：使用更大更多样化数据集而非小规模监督微调。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising approach for general-purpose robot manipulation. However, their generalization is inconsistent: while these models can perform impressively in some settings, fine-tuned variants often fail on novel objects, scenes, and instructions. We apply mechanistic interpretability techniques to better understand the inner workings of VLA models. To probe internal representations, we train Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) on hidden layer activations of the VLA. SAEs learn a sparse dictionary whose features act as a compact, interpretable basis for the model's computation. We find that the large majority of extracted SAE features correspond to memorized sequences from specific training demonstrations. However, some features correspond to interpretable, general, and steerable motion primitives and semantic properties, offering a promising glimpse toward VLA generalizability. We propose a metric to categorize features according to whether they represent generalizable transferable primitives or episode-specific memorization. We validate these findings through steering experiments on the LIBERO benchmark. We show that individual SAE features causally influence robot behavior. Steering general features induces behaviors consistent with their semantic meaning and can be applied across tasks and scenes. This work provides the first mechanistic evidence that VLAs can learn generalizable features across tasks and scenes. We observe that supervised fine-tuning on small robotics datasets disproportionately amplifies memorization. In contrast, training on larger, more diverse datasets (e.g., DROID) or using knowledge insulation promotes more general features. We provide an open-source codebase and user-friendly interface for activation collection, SAE training, and feature steering. Our project page is located at http://drvla.github.io

</details>


### [192] [FASTER: Rethinking Real-Time Flow VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19199)
*Yuxiang Lu,Zhe Liu,Xianzhe Fan,Zhenya Yang,Jinghua Hou,Junyi Li,Kaixin Ding,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FASTER方法通过引入Horizon-Aware Schedule，在流式VLA模型中优先采样近期动作，将反应时间压缩10倍至单步，显著降低机器人实时响应延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步推理方法主要优化轨迹平滑性，但忽视了响应环境变化的关键延迟问题。传统流式VLA模型采用恒定采样计划，迫使系统完成所有采样步骤后才能开始运动，形成反应延迟瓶颈

Method: 提出FASTER方法：1）系统分析反应时间影响因素，发现反应时间服从由首次动作时间和执行视野共同决定的均匀分布；2）引入Horizon-Aware Schedule，在流采样过程中自适应优先处理近期动作；3）结合流式客户端-服务器管道实现

Result: FASTER将立即反应的去噪过程压缩10倍至单步（如在π0.5和X-VLA中），同时保持长视野轨迹质量。在真实机器人上显著降低有效反应延迟，特别是在消费级GPU上部署时。动态乒乓球任务实验证明FASTER实现前所未有的实时响应能力

Conclusion: FASTER通过重新思考动作分块策略中的反应概念，解决了流式VLA模型中的反应延迟瓶颈，为通用策略解锁了前所未有的实时响应能力，能够快速生成准确平滑的轨迹

Abstract: Real-time execution is crucial for deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in the physical world. Existing asynchronous inference methods primarily optimize trajectory smoothness, but neglect the critical latency in reacting to environmental changes. By rethinking the notion of reaction in action chunking policies, this paper presents a systematic analysis of the factors governing reaction time. We show that reaction time follows a uniform distribution determined jointly by the Time to First Action (TTFA) and the execution horizon. Moreover, we reveal that the standard practice of applying a constant schedule in flow-based VLAs can be inefficient and forces the system to complete all sampling steps before any movement can start, forming the bottleneck in reaction latency. To overcome this issue, we propose Fast Action Sampling for ImmediaTE Reaction (FASTER). By introducing a Horizon-Aware Schedule, FASTER adaptively prioritizes near-term actions during flow sampling, compressing the denoising of the immediate reaction by tenfold (e.g., in $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA) into a single step, while preserving the quality of long-horizon trajectory. Coupled with a streaming client-server pipeline, FASTER substantially reduces the effective reaction latency on real robots, especially when deployed on consumer-grade GPUs. Real-world experiments, including a highly dynamic table tennis task, prove that FASTER unlocks unprecedented real-time responsiveness for generalist policies, enabling rapid generation of accurate and smooth trajectories.

</details>


### [193] [OmniVTA: Visuo-Tactile World Modeling for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19201)
*Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Weize Li,Yupeng Zheng,Yujie Zang,Shuai Tian,Xiang Li,Ruihai Wu,Ce Hao,Chen Gao,Si Liu,Haoran Li,Yilun Chen,Shuicheng Yan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniViTac是一个大规模视觉-触觉-动作数据集，包含21,000+轨迹、86个任务和100+物体，分为六种物理交互模式；OmniVTA是基于世界模型的视觉-触觉操作框架，通过预测接触演化和高频触觉反馈实现接触丰富的操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务（如擦拭和装配）需要准确感知接触力、摩擦力变化和状态转换，这些信息无法仅从视觉可靠推断。现有视觉-触觉操作研究面临两个主要限制：数据集规模小、任务覆盖窄；现有方法将触觉信号视为被动观测而非用于建模接触动力学或实现显式闭环控制。

Method: 提出OmniViTac大规模视觉-触觉-动作数据集，包含21,000+轨迹、86个任务、100+物体，组织为六种物理交互模式。基于此提出OmniVTA框架，包含四个紧密耦合模块：自监督触觉编码器、双流视觉-触觉世界模型（预测短时域接触演化）、接触感知融合策略（动作生成）、60Hz反射控制器（闭环校正预测与观测触觉信号偏差）。

Result: 真实机器人实验在所有六种交互类别中显示，OmniVTA优于现有方法，并能良好泛化到未见过的物体和几何配置，证实了将预测接触建模与高频触觉反馈相结合对于接触丰富操作的价值。

Conclusion: OmniViTac数据集和OmniVTA框架通过大规模数据采集和预测接触建模与高频触觉反馈的集成，显著推进了视觉-触觉操作领域，为解决接触丰富任务提供了有效解决方案。所有数据、模型和代码将在项目网站上公开。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation tasks, such as wiping and assembly, require accurate perception of contact forces, friction changes, and state transitions that cannot be reliably inferred from vision alone. Despite growing interest in visuo-tactile manipulation, progress is constrained by two persistent limitations: existing datasets are small in scale and narrow in task coverage, and current methods treat tactile signals as passive observations rather than using them to model contact dynamics or enable closed-loop control explicitly. In this paper, we present \textbf{OmniViTac}, a large-scale visuo-tactile-action dataset comprising $21{,}000+$ trajectories across $86$ tasks and $100+$ objects, organized into six physics-grounded interaction patterns. Building on this dataset, we propose \textbf{OmniVTA}, a world-model-based visuo-tactile manipulation framework that integrates four tightly coupled modules: a self-supervised tactile encoder, a two-stream visuo-tactile world model for predicting short-horizon contact evolution, a contact-aware fusion policy for action generation, and a 60Hz reflexive controller that corrects deviations between predicted and observed tactile signals in a closed loop. Real-robot experiments across all six interaction categories show that OmniVTA outperforms existing methods and generalizes well to unseen objects and geometric configurations, confirming the value of combining predictive contact modeling with high-frequency tactile feedback for contact-rich manipulation. All data, models, and code will be made publicly available on the project website at https://mrsecant.github.io/OmniVTA.

</details>


### [194] [NavTrust: Benchmarking Trustworthiness for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19229)
*Huaide Jiang,Yash Chaudhary,Yuping Wang,Zehao Wang,Raghav Sharma,Manan Mehta,Yang Zhou,Lichao Sun,Zhiwen Fan,Zhengzhong Tu,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavTrust是首个统一评估视觉-语言导航和物体目标导航在现实世界输入模态（RGB、深度、指令）损坏下鲁棒性的基准，揭示了现有方法在真实场景中的严重性能下降，并提出了四种缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航方法主要在理想条件下评估，忽视了现实世界中可能出现的输入模态损坏（如RGB图像噪声、深度传感器误差、指令歧义），这限制了导航系统的实际部署可靠性。

Method: 提出NavTrust统一基准，系统性地对RGB、深度和指令输入进行现实场景的损坏模拟，评估七种SOTA方法在损坏条件下的性能，并测试四种鲁棒性增强策略（包括Uni-NaVid和ETPNav作为基础模型）。

Result: 实验显示现有导航方法在现实损坏下性能显著下降，揭示了关键的鲁棒性缺陷；提出的缓解策略能有效提升对RGB-Depth和指令损坏的鲁棒性，并在真实移动机器人上验证了改进效果。

Conclusion: NavTrust基准填补了导航系统鲁棒性评估的空白，为构建更可信的具身导航系统提供了路线图，强调了在现实世界部署中考虑输入模态损坏的重要性。

Abstract: There are two major categories of embodied navigation: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN), where agents navigate by following natural language instructions; and Object-Goal Navigation (OGN), where agents navigate to a specified target object. However, existing work primarily evaluates model performance under nominal conditions, overlooking the potential corruptions that arise in real-world settings. To address this gap, we present NavTrust, a unified benchmark that systematically corrupts input modalities, including RGB, depth, and instructions, in realistic scenarios and evaluates their impact on navigation performance. To our best knowledge, NavTrust is the first benchmark that exposes embodied navigation agents to diverse RGB-Depth corruptions and instruction variations in a unified framework. Our extensive evaluation of seven state-of-the-art approaches reveals substantial performance degradation under realistic corruptions, which highlights critical robustness gaps and provides a roadmap toward more trustworthy embodied navigation systems. Furthermore, we systematically evaluate four distinct mitigation strategies to enhance robustness against RGB-Depth and instructions corruptions. Our base models include Uni-NaVid and ETPNav. We deployed them on a real mobile robot and observed improved robustness to corruptions. The project website is: https://navtrust.github.io.

</details>


### [195] [Not All Features Are Created Equal: A Mechanistic Study of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19233)
*Bryce Grant,Xijia Zhao,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过激活注入、稀疏自编码器和线性探针等技术，系统分析了6个VLA模型（80M-7B参数）在4个基准上的行为机制，揭示了视觉通路在动作生成中的主导作用、语言敏感性的任务依赖性，以及不同架构中专家通路与VLM通路的功能分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLA模型将感知、语言和运动控制整合到单一架构中，但其如何将多模态输入转化为动作的机制仍不清楚。研究旨在深入理解VLA模型内部表示与行为生成之间的关系，揭示不同架构中视觉、语言和动作通路的交互方式。

Method: 采用激活注入、稀疏自编码器（SAEs）和线性探针三种技术，对6个参数规模从80M到7B的VLA模型进行分析。在4个基准测试上进行了超过394,000次rollout实验，包括跨任务激活注入、零提示注入、子空间注入等方法，并进行了对比性概念识别和因果消融分析。

Result: 1. 视觉通路在所有架构中主导动作生成：基线激活注入能恢复近乎相同的行为，跨任务注入使机器人朝向源任务位置（X-VLA中99.8%的episodes与源轨迹对齐）。2. 语言敏感性取决于任务结构而非模型设计：当视觉上下文唯一指定任务时语言被忽略；当多个目标共享场景时语言变得关键。3. 多通路架构中专家通路编码运动程序，VLM通路编码目标语义。4. 每token SAE处理对大多数架构的动作保真度至关重要。5. 对比识别恢复了82+个操作概念，因果消融显示28-92%的零效应率。

Conclusion: VLA模型的动作生成机制具有系统性规律：视觉通路编码空间绑定的运动程序，语言敏感性是任务依赖的，多通路架构中功能分离明显。这些发现为理解VLA模型内部表示提供了实证基础，并发布了Action Atlas工具供交互式探索VLA表示。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models combine perception, language, and motor control in a single architecture, yet how they translate multimodal inputs into actions remains poorly understood. We apply activation injection, sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and linear probes to six models spanning 80M--7B parameters across 394,000+ rollout episodes on four benchmarks. The visual pathway dominates action generation across all architectures: injecting baseline activations into null-prompt episodes recovers near-identical behavior, while cross-task injection steers robots toward source-task positions (99.8\% of X-VLA episodes align with the source trajectory), exposing spatially bound motor programs tied to scene coordinates rather than abstract task representations. Language sensitivity depends on task structure, not model design: when visual context uniquely specifies the task, language is ignored; when multiple goals share a scene, language becomes essential (X-VLA \texttt{libero\_goal}: 94\%$\to$10\% under wrong prompts vs.\ \texttt{libero\_object}: 60--100\% regardless). In all three multi-pathway architectures (\pizhalf{}, SmolVLA, GR00T), expert pathways encode motor programs while VLM pathways encode goal semantics ($2\times$ greater behavioral displacement from expert injection), and subspace injection confirms these occupy separable activation subspaces. Per-token SAE processing is essential for action fidelity on most architectures, though mean-pooling improves fidelity on X-VLA. Contrastive identification recovers 82+ manipulation concepts, and causal ablation reveals sensitivity spanning 28--92\% zero-effect rates independent of representation width. We release \textbf{Action Atlas} (https://action-atlas.com) for interactive exploration of VLA representations across all six models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [DEAF: A Benchmark for Diagnostic Evaluation of Acoustic Faithfulness in Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18048)
*Jiaqi Xiong,Yunjia Qi,Qi Cao,Yu Zheng,Weisheng Xu,Ziteng Wang,Ruofan Liao,Yutong Zhang,Sichen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DEAF基准测试，用于诊断音频多模态大语言模型是否真正处理声学信号而非依赖文本语义推理，发现现有模型存在文本主导偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有音频多模态大语言模型在语音基准测试中表现出色，但尚不清楚这些模型是否真正处理声学信号，还是主要依赖文本语义推理。需要系统研究模型的声学忠实性。

Method: 提出DEAF基准测试，包含2700多个冲突刺激，涵盖情感韵律、背景声音和说话人身份三个声学维度。设计多级评估框架，逐步增加文本影响，从内容语义冲突到误导提示及其组合。引入诊断指标量化模型对文本线索而非声学信号的依赖程度。

Result: 评估七个音频多模态大语言模型发现一致的文本主导模式：模型对声学变化敏感，但预测主要由文本输入驱动，揭示了标准语音基准测试的高性能与真正声学理解之间的差距。

Conclusion: 现有音频多模态大语言模型存在文本主导偏差，虽然对声学变化敏感，但预测主要依赖文本线索而非声学信号，表明需要更严格的评估方法来确保模型真正理解声学信息。

Abstract: Recent Audio Multimodal Large Language Models (Audio MLLMs) demonstrate impressive performance on speech benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether these models genuinely process acoustic signals or rely on text-based semantic inference. To systematically study this question, we introduce DEAF (Diagnostic Evaluation of Acoustic Faithfulness), a benchmark of over 2,700 conflict stimuli spanning three acoustic dimensions: emotional prosody, background sounds, and speaker identity. Then, we design a controlled multi-level evaluation framework that progressively increases textual influence, ranging from semantic conflicts in the content to misleading prompts and their combination, allowing us to disentangle content-driven bias from prompt-induced sycophancy. We further introduce diagnostic metrics to quantify model reliance on textual cues over acoustic signals. Our evaluation of seven Audio MLLMs reveals a consistent pattern of text dominance: models are sensitive to acoustic variations, yet predictions are predominantly driven by textual inputs, revealing a gap between high performance on standard speech benchmarks and genuine acoustic understanding.

</details>


### [197] [Continually self-improving AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18073)
*Zitong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出三种方法突破当前语言模型依赖人类创造者的限制：1）通过合成数据方法提高小规模专业语料的知识获取效率；2）利用固定人类数据自生成合成数据进行预训练；3）通过测试时算法搜索超越人工设计的训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于语言模型的AI系统虽然强大，但其能力仍受限于人类创造者：1）从小规模专业语料获取新知识的数据效率低下；2）训练依赖有限的人类生成历史数据；3）训练流程受限于人类研究者能发现的算法。论文旨在突破这些限制，实现持续自我改进的AI。

Method: 提出三个核心方法：1）合成数据方法：将小规模语料多样化和放大为丰富的知识表示，使模型能从有限源材料有效更新参数；2）自生成合成数据：在固定人类数据基础上，模型自生成合成数据来引导基础预训练能力，无需从现成指令调优模型蒸馏；3）测试时算法搜索：通过扩展测试时在算法空间上的搜索，使AI能探索比人类研究者手动探索更大的学习算法配置空间。

Result: 论文展示了三种方法在突破语言模型依赖人类限制方面的有效性：1）合成数据方法提高了小规模知识获取的数据效率；2）自生成合成数据方法减少了模型对人类数据的依赖；3）测试时算法搜索使AI能超越人工设计的训练范式，实现更广泛的算法探索。

Conclusion: 该论文通过三个章节的研究，在创建持续自我改进的AI方面迈出了一小步，突破了当前语言模型在知识获取效率、人类数据依赖和人工训练范式方面的限制，为实现更自主、更高效的AI系统提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Modern language model-based AI systems are remarkably powerful, yet their capabilities remain fundamentally capped by their human creators in three key ways. First, although a model's weights can be updated via fine-tuning, acquiring new knowledge from small, specialized corpora after pretraining remains highly data-inefficient. Second, the training of these systems relies heavily on finite, human-generated data from across history. Third, the pipelines used to train AI models are confined by the algorithms that human researchers can discover and explore. This thesis takes a small step toward overcoming these inherent limitations, presenting three chapters aimed at breaking these dependencies to create continually self-improving AI. First, to overcome this data-efficiency barrier in knowledge acquisition, we propose a synthetic data approach that diversifies and amplifies small corpora into rich knowledge representations, enabling a model to effectively update its parameters from limited source material. Second, to reduce reliance on human data, we show that given a fixed amount of such data, the model can self-generate synthetic data to bootstrap its fundamental pretraining capabilities without distillation from any off-the-shelf, instruction-tuned LM. Finally, to transcend human-engineered training paradigms, we demonstrate that by scaling search during test time over the space of algorithms, AI can search over a larger space of learning algorithm configurations than human researchers can explore manually.

</details>


### [198] [Multi-Trait Subspace Steering to Reveal the Dark Side of Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18085)
*Xin Wei Chia,Swee Liang Wong,Jonathan Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发MultiTraitsss框架生成具有累积有害行为模式的Dark模型，用于研究人类-AI交互中的心理危害机制并提出保护措施


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类-AI交互中出现负面心理后果（包括心理健康危机和用户伤害）的风险日益增加，但研究有害交互机制面临方法学挑战，因为有机有害交互通常需要持续参与和大量对话上下文，难以在受控环境中模拟

Method: 开发Multi-Trait Subspace Steering (MultiTraitsss)框架，利用已建立的危机相关特征和新的子空间引导框架生成展示累积有害行为模式的Dark模型

Result: 单轮和多轮评估显示，Dark模型能持续产生有害交互和结果；基于Dark模型提出了减少人类-AI交互中有害结果的保护措施

Conclusion: MultiTraitsss框架为解决研究人类-AI有害交互的方法学挑战提供了有效工具，生成的Dark模型可用于开发保护措施以减少交互风险

Abstract: Recent incidents have highlighted alarming cases where human-AI interactions led to negative psychological outcomes, including mental health crises and even user harm. As LLMs serve as sources of guidance, emotional support, and even informal therapy, these risks are poised to escalate. However, studying the mechanisms underlying harmful human-AI interactions presents significant methodological challenges, where organic harmful interactions typically develop over sustained engagement, requiring extensive conversational context that are difficult to simulate in controlled settings. To address this gap, we developed a Multi-Trait Subspace Steering (MultiTraitsss) framework that leverages established crisis-associated traits and novel subspace steering framework to generate Dark models that exhibits cumulative harmful behavioral patterns. Single-turn and multi-turn evaluations show that our dark models consistently produce harmful interaction and outcomes. Using our Dark models, we propose protective measure to reduce harmful outcomes in Human-AI interactions.

</details>


### [199] [Adaptive Domain Models: Bayesian Evolution, Warm Rotation, and Principled Training for Geometric and Neuromorphic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18104)
*Houston Haynes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于几何代数、确定内存管理和posit算术的新型AI训练架构，实现深度无关的训练内存（约2倍推理内存）、保级权重更新和精确梯度累积，支持贝叶斯蒸馏和热旋转部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI训练基于IEEE-754算术的反向模式自动微分，导致训练内存开销大、优化器复杂、几何特性退化。需要一种新的算术基础来解决这些问题。

Method: 结合三个关键技术：1) 维度类型系统和确定性内存管理框架，实现栈可分配梯度和精确quire累积；2) 程序超图，通过几何代数计算保持grade不变性；3) b-posit 2026标准，使posit算术在传统推理硬件上可行。引入贝叶斯蒸馏和热旋转部署机制。

Result: 实现深度无关的训练内存（约2倍推理内存）、保级权重更新、精确梯度累积，支持损失函数优化和脉冲时序依赖的神经形态模型。通过贝叶斯蒸馏解决领域特定训练的数据稀缺问题，通过热旋转实现无服务中断的模型更新。

Conclusion: 该架构产生一类领域特定的AI系统，比通用模型更小更精确，具有持续适应性、可验证正确性，并能从现有模型初始化，解决了传统训练架构的内存、几何特性和部署问题。

Abstract: Prevailing AI training infrastructure assumes reverse-mode automatic differentiation over IEEE-754 arithmetic. The memory overhead of training relative to inference, optimizer complexity, and structural degradation of geometric properties through training are consequences of this arithmetic substrate. This paper develops an alternative training architecture grounded in three prior results: the Dimensional Type System and Deterministic Memory Management framework [6], which establishes stack-eligible gradient allocation and exact quire accumulation as design-time verifiable properties; the Program Hypergraph [8], which establishes grade preservation through geometric algebra computations as a type-level invariant; and the b-posit 2026 standard [10], which makes posit arithmetic tractable across hardware targets conventionally considered inference-only. Their composition enables depth-independent training memory bounded to approximately twice the inference footprint, grade-preserving weight updates, and exact gradient accumulation, applicable uniformly to loss-function-optimized and spike-timing-dependent neuromorphic models. We introduce Bayesian distillation, a mechanism by which the latent prior structure of a general-purpose model is extracted through the ADM training regime, resolving the data-scarcity bootstrapping problem for domain-specific training. For deployment, we introduce warm rotation, an operational pattern in which an updated model transitions into an active inference pathway without service interruption, with structural correctness formalized through PHG certificates and signed version records. The result is a class of domain-specific AI systems that are smaller and more precise than general-purpose models, continuously adaptive, verifiably correct with respect to the physical structure of their domains, and initializable from existing models.

</details>


### [200] [Efficient Dense Crowd Trajectory Prediction Via Dynamic Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18166)
*Antonius Bima Murti Wijaya,Paul Henderson,Marwa Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于聚类的群体轨迹预测方法，通过将个体按相似属性分组来加速处理，降低计算成本，同时保持预测精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法主要关注个体轨迹预测，但在密集人群场景中面临计算成本高、跟踪输出噪声大和不准确等问题，需要更高效的解决方案

Method: 提出基于聚类的方法，将具有相似属性的个体随时间分组，通过群体摘要实现快速执行；该方法为即插即用方案，可与现有轨迹预测器结合，用聚类中心代替原始行人输入

Result: 在多个密集人群场景中验证，相比现有方法实现了更快的处理速度和更低的内存使用，同时保持了预测精度

Conclusion: 基于聚类的群体轨迹预测方法能有效解决密集人群场景中的计算效率问题，为公共安全和管理提供更实用的解决方案

Abstract: Crowd trajectory prediction plays a crucial role in public safety and management, where it can help prevent disasters such as stampedes. Recent works address the problem by predicting individual trajectories and considering surrounding objects based on manually annotated data. However, these approaches tend to overlook dense crowd scenarios, where the challenges of automation become more pronounced due to the massiveness, noisiness, and inaccuracy of the tracking outputs, resulting in high computational costs. To address these challenges, we propose and extensively evaluate a novel cluster-based approach that groups individuals based on similar attributes over time, enabling faster execution through accurate group summarisation. Our plug-and-play method can be combined with existing trajectory predictors by using our output centroid in place of their pedestrian input. We evaluate our proposed method on several challenging dense crowd scenes. We demonstrated that our approach leads to faster processing and lower memory usage when compared with state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining the accuracy

</details>


### [201] [TeachingCoach: A Fine-Tuned Scaffolding Chatbot for Instructional Guidance to Instructors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18189)
*Isabel Molnar,Peiyu Li,Si Chen,Sugana Chawla,James Lang,Ronald Metoyer,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TeachingCoach是一个基于教学法的聊天机器人，通过实时对话指导支持高校教师专业发展，相比GPT-4o mini提供更清晰、更具反思性和响应性的指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高校教师缺乏及时且基于教学法的支持，现有工具要么依赖通用聊天机器人建议，要么依赖不可扩展的教学中心人工咨询，需要一种可扩展的教学指导解决方案。

Method: 采用数据驱动的流程：从教育资源中提取教学法规则，使用合成对话生成来微调专门的语言模型，引导教师完成问题识别、诊断和策略开发。

Result: 专家评估显示TeachingCoach比GPT-4o mini基线产生更清晰、更具反思性和响应性的指导；用户研究揭示了对话深度与交互效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 基于教学法的合成数据驱动聊天机器人可以改善教学支持，为未来教学聊天机器人系统提供可扩展的设计方法。

Abstract: Higher education instructors often lack timely and pedagogically grounded support, as scalable instructional guidance remains limited and existing tools rely on generic chatbot advice or non-scalable teaching center human-human consultations. We present TeachingCoach, a pedagogically grounded chatbot designed to support instructor professional development through real-time, conversational guidance. TeachingCoach is built on a data-centric pipeline that extracts pedagogical rules from educational resources and uses synthetic dialogue generation to fine-tune a specialized language model that guides instructors through problem identification, diagnosis, and strategy development. Expert evaluations show TeachingCoach produces clearer, more reflective, and more responsive guidance than a GPT-4o mini baseline, while a user study with higher education instructors highlights trade-offs between conversational depth and interaction efficiency. Together, these results demonstrate that pedagogically grounded, synthetic data driven chatbots can improve instructional support and offer a scalable design approach for future instructional chatbot systems.

</details>


### [202] [A Computationally Efficient Learning of Artificial Intelligence System Reliability Considering Error Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18201)
*Fenglian Pan,Yinwei Zhang,Yili Hong,Larry Head,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于物理仿真的自动驾驶AI系统可靠性建模框架，解决错误传播量化难题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧城市中AI系统可靠性至关重要，但存在数据稀缺、模型假设不成立、计算复杂三大挑战，需要量化错误在功能阶段间的传播

Method: 使用基于物理的自动驾驶仿真平台和可验证错误注入器生成高质量数据，开发显式表征错误传播的可靠性建模框架，采用计算高效的复合似然期望最大化算法估计参数

Result: 应用于自动驾驶感知系统的可靠性建模，证明了方法的预测准确性和计算效率

Conclusion: 提出的仿真数据生成和建模框架有效解决了AI系统可靠性分析中的数据稀缺、模型有效性和计算复杂度问题，为复杂AI系统可靠性评估提供了可行方案

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly prominent in emerging smart cities, yet their reliability remains a critical concern. These systems typically operate through a sequence of interconnected functional stages, where upstream errors may propagate to downstream stages, ultimately affecting overall system reliability. Quantifying such error propagation is essential for accurate modeling of AI system reliability. However, this task is challenging due to: i) data availability: real-world AI system reliability data are often scarce and constrained by privacy concerns; ii) model validity: recurring error events across sequential stages are interdependent, violating the independence assumptions of statistical inference; and iii) computational complexity: AI systems process large volumes of high-speed data, resulting in frequent and complex recurrent error events that are difficult to track and analyze. To address these challenges, this paper leverages a physics-based autonomous vehicle simulation platform with a justifiable error injector to generate high-quality data for AI system reliability analysis. Building on this data, a new reliability modeling framework is developed to explicitly characterize error propagation across stages. Model parameters are estimated using a computationally efficient, theoretically guaranteed composite likelihood expectation - maximization algorithm. Its application to the reliability modeling for autonomous vehicle perception systems demonstrates its predictive accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [203] [Retrieval-Augmented LLM Agents: Learning to Learn from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18272)
*Thomas Palmeira Ferraz,Romain Deffayet,Vassilina Nikoulina,Hervé Déjean,Stéphane Clinchant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合微调和经验检索的方法来增强LLM智能体的泛化能力，通过系统研究检索增强LLM智能体的训练策略，显著提升了未见任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在未见任务上的泛化能力有限，现有方法（微调或基于检索的经验增强）各有局限：微调难以泛化到新任务，而经验检索通常弱于监督基线。需要结合两者优势来提升智能体的学习能力。

Method: 1. 建立基于LoRA的稳健监督微调(SFT)配方；2. 系统分析经验检索的关键设计选择（存储、查询、轨迹选择）；3. 提出将经验检索集成到微调流程中的完整管道。

Result: 1. 提出的SFT配方优于多个SOTA智能体训练管道；2. 确定了经验检索的最优策略；3. 结合方法显著提升了智能体在未见任务上的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 结合微调和经验检索的方法为构建能够从经验中学习的智能体提供了可扩展且有效的框架，通过系统化的训练策略显著改善了泛化能力。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have advanced the development of general-purpose agents, achieving robust generalization to unseen tasks remains a significant challenge. Current approaches typically rely on either fine-tuning or training-free memory-augmented generation using retrieved experience; yet both have limitations: fine-tuning often fails to extrapolate to new tasks, while experience retrieval often underperforms compared to supervised baselines. In this work, we propose to combine these approaches and systematically study how to train retrieval-augmented LLM agents to effectively leverage retrieved trajectories in-context. First, we establish a robust supervised fine-tuning (SFT) recipe using LoRA that outperforms several state-of-the-art agent training pipelines. Second, we provide a detailed analysis of key design choices for experience retrieval, identifying optimal strategies for storage, querying, and trajectory selection. Finally, we propose a pipeline that integrates experience retrieval into the fine-tuning process. Our results demonstrate that this combined approach significantly improves generalization to unseen tasks, providing a scalable and effective framework for building agents that learn to learn from experience.

</details>


### [204] [EDM-ARS: A Domain-Specific Multi-Agent System for Automated Educational Data Mining Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18273)
*Chenguang Pan,Zhou Zhang,Weixuan Xiao,Chengyuan Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EDM-ARS是一个教育数据挖掘自动化研究系统，采用多智能体管道实现端到端的EDM研究自动化，特别专注于预测建模任务，能够生成完整的LaTeX论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个领域特定的自动化研究管道，将教育专业知识嵌入到研究生命周期的每个阶段，以自动化教育数据挖掘研究过程，支持教育研究社区。

Method: 采用多智能体架构，包含五个专门的LLM驱动智能体（问题制定者、数据工程师、分析师、评论家、作者），通过状态机协调器管理，支持修订循环、检查点恢复和沙箱代码执行，使用三层数据注册表设计编码教育领域专业知识。

Result: 系统能够根据研究提示和数据集生成完整的LaTeX手稿，包含真实的Semantic Scholar引用、经过验证的机器学习分析以及自动化的方法论同行评审，已作为开源项目发布。

Conclusion: EDM-ARS作为领域感知自动化研究管道的通用框架首次实例化，虽然存在单数据集范围和公式化输出等限制，但为向因果推断、迁移学习、心理测量和多数据集泛化的扩展提供了路线图。

Abstract: In this technical report, we present the Educational Data Mining Automated Research System (EDM-ARS), a domain-specific multi-agent pipeline that automates end-to-end educational data mining (EDM) research. We conceptualize EDM-ARS as a general framework for domain-aware automated research pipelines, where educational expertise is embedded into each stage of the research lifecycle. As a first instantiation of this framework, we focus on predictive modeling tasks. Within this scope, EDM-ARS orchestrates five specialized LLM-powered agents (ProblemFormulator, DataEngineer, Analyst, Critic, and Writer) through a state-machine coordinator that supports revision loops, checkpoint-based recovery, and sandboxed code execution. Given a research prompt and a dataset, EDM-ARS produces a complete LaTeX manuscript with real Semantic Scholar citations, validated machine learning analyses, and automated methodological peer review. We also provide a detailed description of the system architecture, the three-tier data registry design that encodes educational domain expertise, the specification of each agent, the inter-agent communication protocol, and mechanisms for error-handling and self-correction. Finally, we discuss current limitations, including single-dataset scope and formulaic paper output, and outline a phased roadmap toward causal inference, transfer learning, psychometric, and multi-dataset generalization. EDM-ARS is released as an open-source project to support the educational research community.

</details>


### [205] [CORE: Robust Out-of-Distribution Detection via Confidence and Orthogonal Residual Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18290)
*Jin Mo Yang,Hyung-Sin Kim,Saewoong Bahk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CORE提出了一种新的OOD检测方法，通过解耦置信度信号和残差信号，在分类器对齐子空间和残差子空间中独立评分并归一化求和，实现了跨架构和数据集的一致高性能检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法存在不一致性问题：基于logit的方法只能看到分类器的置信度信号，而基于特征的方法试图测量训练分布中的成员关系，但在完整特征空间中置信度和成员关系纠缠在一起，继承了架构敏感的失败模式。需要一种能跨不同架构和数据集一致工作的鲁棒检测方法。

Method: CORE方法观察到倒数第二层特征自然分解为两个正交子空间：分类器对齐组件（编码置信度）和分类器丢弃的残差。发现残差携带了in-distribution数据的类特定方向特征（成员关系信号）。CORE通过在每个子空间中独立评分，然后通过归一化求和将它们组合起来，解耦了这两个信号。

Result: CORE在五种架构和五种基准配置上实现了竞争性或最先进的性能，在五个设置中的三个中排名第一，获得了最高的平均AUROC，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过解耦置信度信号和残差信号，CORE方法能够利用这两个正交子空间中的独立信息，产生鲁棒的OOD检测，在单一方法不可靠的情况下仍能保持良好性能，解决了现有方法跨架构和数据集不一致的问题。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential for deploying deep learning models reliably, yet no single method performs consistently across architectures and datasets -- a scorer that leads on one benchmark often falters on another. We attribute this inconsistency to a shared structural limitation: logit-based methods see only the classifier's confidence signal, while feature-based methods attempt to measure membership in the training distribution but do so in the full feature space where confidence and membership are entangled, inheriting architecture-sensitive failure modes. We observe that penultimate features naturally decompose into two orthogonal subspaces: a classifier-aligned component encoding confidence, and a residual the classifier discards. We discover that this residual carries a class-specific directional signature for in-distribution data -- a membership signal invisible to logit-based methods and entangled with noise in feature-based methods. We propose CORE (COnfidence + REsidual), which disentangles the two signals by scoring each subspace independently and combines them via normalized summation. Because the two signals are orthogonal by construction, their failure modes are approximately independent, producing robust detection where either view alone is unreliable. CORE achieves competitive or state-of-the-art performance across five architectures and five benchmark configurations, ranking first in three of five settings and achieving the highest grand average AUROC with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [206] [The Validity Gap in Health AI Evaluation: A Cross-Sectional Analysis of Benchmark Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18294)
*Alvin Rajkomar,Pavan Sudarshan,Angela Lai,Lily Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现医疗大语言模型评估基准存在"有效性差距"：基准测试内容与真实临床需求脱节，缺乏原始临床数据、弱势群体代表性不足，且缺少安全关键场景和慢性病管理内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床研究依赖透明的纳入标准以确保可推广性，而验证医疗相关大语言模型的基准测试很少描述其包含的"患者"或"查询"人群特征。没有明确的组成描述，聚合性能指标可能误导模型临床准备度的评估。

Method: 分析了六个公共基准测试中的18,707个消费者健康查询，使用大语言模型作为自动编码工具，应用标准化的16字段分类法来分析上下文、主题和意图。

Result: 发现结构性"有效性差距"：基准测试虽从静态检索发展到交互对话，但临床组成仍与真实需求脱节。42%语料引用客观数据，但偏向健康可穿戴信号(17.7%)；复杂诊断输入罕见，包括实验室值(5.2%)、影像(3.8%)和原始医疗记录(0.6%)。安全关键场景几乎缺失：自杀/自残查询<0.7%，慢性病管理仅5.5%。基准测试也忽视了弱势群体(儿科/老年人<11%)和全球健康需求。

Conclusion: 评估基准仍与真实临床需求脱节，缺乏原始临床数据、弱势群体充分代表性以及纵向慢性护理场景。该领域必须采用标准化查询分析——类似于临床试验报告——以使评估与临床实践的全部复杂性保持一致。

Abstract: Background: Clinical trials rely on transparent inclusion criteria to ensure generalizability. In contrast, benchmarks validating health-related large language models (LLMs) rarely characterize the "patient" or "query" populations they contain. Without defined composition, aggregate performance metrics may misrepresent model readiness for clinical use.
  Methods: We analyzed 18,707 consumer health queries across six public benchmarks using LLMs as automated coding instruments to apply a standardized 16-field taxonomy profiling context, topic, and intent.
  Results: We identified a structural "validity gap." While benchmarks have evolved from static retrieval to interactive dialogue, clinical composition remains misaligned with real-world needs. Although 42% of the corpus referenced objective data, this was polarized toward wellness-focused wearable signals (17.7%); complex diagnostic inputs remained rare, including laboratory values (5.2%), imaging (3.8%), and raw medical records (0.6%). Safety-critical scenarios were effectively absent: suicide/self-harm queries comprised <0.7% of the corpus and chronic disease management only 5.5%. Benchmarks also neglected vulnerable populations (pediatrics/older adults <11%) and global health needs.
  Conclusions: Evaluation benchmarks remain misaligned with real-world clinical needs, lacking raw clinical artifacts, adequate representation of vulnerable populations, and longitudinal chronic care scenarios. The field must adopt standardized query profiling--analogous to clinical trial reporting--to align evaluation with the full complexity of clinical practice.

</details>


### [207] [Consumer-to-Clinical Language Shifts in Ambient AI Draft Notes and Clinician-Finalized Documentation: A Multi-level Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18327)
*Ha Na Cho,Yawen Guo,Sairam Sutari,Emilie Chow,Steven Tam,Danielle Perret,Deepti Pandita,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究量化了临床医生如何将AI生成的、使用通俗语言的临床笔记草稿修改为标准化临床术语，发现编辑显著减少了术语密度，其中评估与计划部分转换量最大，不同医生编辑强度存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境AI根据医患对话生成临床笔记草稿时，常使用通俗或面向患者的表达方式而非标准化临床术语。临床医生如何将这些草稿修改为符合专业文档规范尚不清楚，需要量化分析这种从消费者语言到临床术语的转换过程。

Method: 使用字典确认的转换框架量化临床医生的编辑行为。分析了34,726次就诊中的71,173对AI草稿和最终笔记部分对。将"确认转换"定义为在同一部分中用字典映射的临床等效术语替换消费者表达。

Result: 编辑显著降低了所有部分的术语密度(p<0.001)。评估与计划部分转换量最大(59.3%)。共识别出4,114个笔记部分中的7,576次转换事件(5.8%)，占消费者术语删除的1.2%。不同临床医生的转换强度存在显著差异(p<0.001)。

Conclusion: 临床医生的后期编辑显示出从对话式表达向标准化、适合各部分的临床术语的一致转变，这支持了需要具备部分感知能力的环境AI设计。

Abstract: Ambient AI generates draft clinical notes from patient-clinician conversations, often using lay or consumer-oriented phrasing to support patient understanding instead of standardized clinical terminology. How clinicians revise these drafts for professional documentation conventions remains unclear. We quantified clinician editing for consumer-to- clinical normalization using a dictionary-confirmed transformation framework. We analyzed 71,173 AI-draft and finalized-note section pairs from 34,726 encounters. Confirmed transformations were defined as replacing a consumer expression with its dictionary-mapped clinical equivalent in the same section. Editing significantly reduced terminology density across all sections (p < 0.001). The Assessment and Plan accounted for the largest transformation volume (59.3%). Our analysis identified 7,576 transformation events across 4,114 note sections (5.8%), representing 1.2% consumer-term deletions. Transformation intensity varied across individual clinicians (p < 0.001). Overall, clinician post-editing demonstrates consistent shifts from conversational phrasing toward standardized, section- appropriate clinical terminology, supporting section-aware ambient AI design.

</details>


### [208] [FaithSteer-BENCH: A Deployment-Aligned Stress-Testing Benchmark for Inference-Time Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18329)
*Zikang Ding,Qiying Hu,Yi Zhang,Hongji Li,Junchi Yao,Hongbo Liu,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FaithSteer-BENCH是一个压力测试基准，用于评估LLM推理时引导方法在部署环境下的可靠性，揭示了现有方法在可控性、能力保持和鲁棒性方面的系统性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常认为推理时引导是轻量级、参数无关的LLM行为控制机制，但这些结论是在相对宽松的评估设置下得出的，忽视了部署约束、能力权衡和实际鲁棒性。需要更严格的评估框架来检验引导方法在真实部署环境下的可靠性。

Method: 提出FaithSteer-BENCH基准，通过三个门控标准评估引导方法：可控性、效用保持和鲁棒性。该基准在固定部署式操作点进行压力测试，涵盖多种模型和代表性引导方法，并分析机制层面的诊断指标。

Result: 研究发现现有方法在标准评估中被掩盖的系统性失败模式：虚幻可控性、对无关能力的可测量认知税、在轻微指令级扰动、角色提示、编码变换和数据稀缺下的显著脆弱性。门控基准结果显示现有方法在部署导向的实际设置中不一定提供可靠可控性。

Conclusion: FaithSteer-BENCH为未来引导方法设计、可靠性评估和部署导向研究提供了统一基准和更清晰的分析视角，揭示了现有方法在实际部署中的局限性，并指出许多方法仅诱导提示条件对齐而非稳定的潜在方向性转变。

Abstract: Inference-time steering is widely regarded as a lightweight and parameter-free mechanism for controlling large language model (LLM) behavior, and prior work has often suggested that simple activation-level interventions can reliably induce targeted behavioral changes. However, such conclusions are typically drawn under relatively relaxed evaluation settings that overlook deployment constraints, capability trade-offs, and real-world robustness. We therefore introduce \textbf{FaithSteer-BENCH}, a stress-testing benchmark that evaluates steering methods at a fixed deployment-style operating point through three gate-wise criteria: controllability, utility preservation, and robustness. Across multiple models and representative steering approaches, we uncover several systematic failure modes that are largely obscured under standard evaluation, including illusory controllability, measurable cognitive tax on unrelated capabilities, and substantial brittleness under mild instruction-level perturbations, role prompts, encoding transformations, and data scarcity. Gate-wise benchmark results show that existing methods do not necessarily provide reliable controllability in deployment-oriented practical settings. In addition, mechanism-level diagnostics indicate that many steering methods induce prompt-conditional alignment rather than stable latent directional shifts, further explaining their fragility under stress. FaithSteer-BENCH therefore provides a unified benchmark and a clearer analytical lens for future method design, reliability evaluation, and deployment-oriented research in steering.

</details>


### [209] [Interpretability without actionability: mechanistic methods cannot correct language model errors despite near-perfect internal representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18353)
*Sanjay Basu,Sadiq Y. Patel,Parth Sheth,Bhairavi Muralidharan,Namrata Elamaran,Aakriti Kinra,John Morgan,Rajaie Batniji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统测试了四种机制可解释性方法在弥合语言模型内部知识与输出性能之间差距的效果，发现当前方法无法可靠地将内部知识转化为纠正后的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在内部表示中编码的任务相关知识远超其输出性能，但机制可解释性方法是否能弥合这一知识-行动差距尚未得到系统测试。研究者旨在评估不同可解释性方法在纠正临床决策错误方面的有效性。

Method: 研究比较了四种机制可解释性方法：概念瓶颈引导（Steerling-8B）、稀疏自编码器特征引导、logit lens结合激活修补、以及线性探测结合真实性分离向量引导（Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct）。使用400个医生判定的临床案例（144个危险案例，256个良性案例）测试这些方法纠正假阴性分诊错误的能力。

Result: 线性探测能以98.2%的AUROC区分危险与良性案例，但模型输出灵敏度仅为45.1%，存在53个百分点的知识-行动差距。概念瓶颈引导纠正了20%的漏检危险，但破坏了53%的正确检测，效果与随机扰动无差异（p=0.84）。SAE特征引导尽管有3,695个显著特征却产生零效果。TSV引导在高强度下纠正了24%的漏检危险，同时仅破坏6%的正确检测，但仍有76%的错误未纠正。

Conclusion: 当前机制可解释性方法无法可靠地将内部知识转化为纠正后的输出，这对假设可解释性能实现有效错误纠正的AI安全框架具有重要影响。

Abstract: Language models encode task-relevant knowledge in internal representations that far exceeds their output performance, but whether mechanistic interpretability methods can bridge this knowledge-action gap has not been systematically tested. We compared four mechanistic interpretability methods -- concept bottleneck steering (Steerling-8B), sparse autoencoder feature steering, logit lens with activation patching, and linear probing with truthfulness separator vector steering (Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct) -- for correcting false-negative triage errors using 400 physician-adjudicated clinical vignettes (144 hazards, 256 benign). Linear probes discriminated hazardous from benign cases with 98.2% AUROC, yet the model's output sensitivity was only 45.1%, a 53-percentage-point knowledge-action gap. Concept bottleneck steering corrected 20% of missed hazards but disrupted 53% of correct detections, indistinguishable from random perturbation (p=0.84). SAE feature steering produced zero effect despite 3,695 significant features. TSV steering at high strength corrected 24% of missed hazards while disrupting 6% of correct detections, but left 76% of errors uncorrected. Current mechanistic interpretability methods cannot reliably translate internal knowledge into corrected outputs, with implications for AI safety frameworks that assume interpretability enables effective error correction.

</details>


### [210] [LGESynthNet: Controlled Scar Synthesis for Improved Scar Segmentation in Cardiac LGE-MRI Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18356)
*Athira J. Jacob,Puneet Sharma,Daniel Rueckert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LGESynthNet：基于潜在扩散模型的LGE心脏MRI增强区域可控合成框架，仅需少量训练数据即可生成解剖学一致且条件可控的合成图像，用于数据增强提升下游分割和检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGE心脏MRI增强区域分割对诊断缺血性和非缺血性心肌病至关重要，但像素级标注耗时费力导致标注数据稀缺。现有生成模型通常需要大量训练数据且难以精确控制局部特征。

Method: 提出LGESynthNet框架，基于ControlNet架构的潜在扩散模型，通过修复任务实现增强区域的可控合成。包含三个关键组件：条件特定监督的奖励模型、解剖描述性文本提示的标注模块、生物医学文本编码器。仅使用429张图像（79名患者）训练。

Result: 模型能生成真实且解剖学一致的合成样本。通过质量控制过滤器筛选条件保真度高的输出，用于训练增强后，下游分割和检测性能分别提升高达6个点和20个点。

Conclusion: LGESynthNet展示了在小规模数据集上实现可控增强合成的可行性，通过合成数据增强显著提升下游任务性能，为医学图像分析提供了有效的数据增强解决方案。

Abstract: Segmentation of enhancement in LGE cardiac MRI is critical for diagnosing various ischemic and non-ischemic cardiomyopathies. However, creating pixel-level annotations for these images is challenging and labor-intensive, leading to limited availability of annotated data. Generative models, particularly diffusion models, offer promise for synthetic data generation, yet many rely on large training datasets and often struggle with fine-grained conditioning control, especially for small or localized features. We introduce LGESynthNet, a latent diffusion-based framework for controllable enhancement synthesis, enabling explicit control over size, location, and transmural extent. Formulated as inpainting using a ControlNet-based architecture, the model integrates: (a) a reward model for conditioning-specific supervision, (b) a captioning module for anatomically descriptive text prompts, and (c) a biomedical text encoder. Trained on just 429 images (79 patients), it produces realistic, anatomically coherent samples. A quality control filter selects outputs with high conditioning-fidelity, which when used for training augmentation, improve downstream segmentation and detection performance, by up-to 6 and 20 points respectively.

</details>


### [211] [From Weak Cues to Real Identities: Evaluating Inference-Driven De-Anonymization in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18382)
*Myeongseob Ko,Jihyun Jeong,Sumiran Singh Thakur,Gyuhak Kim,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM智能体能够从分散的非识别性线索中自主重构真实世界身份，这种推理驱动的链接威胁显著削弱了传统匿名化的保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统匿名化依赖重新识别成本高（需要领域专业知识、定制算法和人工验证）作为实际保护措施。然而，LLM智能体的出现可能削弱这一屏障，因为它们能够从分散的非识别性线索中自主重构身份，形成新的隐私风险。

Method: 将威胁形式化为"推理驱动的链接"，并在三个场景中系统评估：经典链接场景（Netflix和AOL）、InferLink（控制基准，变化任务意图、共享线索和攻击者知识）以及现代文本丰富的工件。使用LLM智能体执行固定池匹配和开放式身份解析，无需任务特定启发式方法。

Result: 在Netflix Prize设置中，智能体重构了79.2%的身份，显著优于56.0%的经典基线。链接不仅出现在明确的对抗性提示下，也作为InferLink中良性跨源分析和非结构化研究叙述的副产品出现。

Conclusion: 身份推理（而不仅仅是明确的信息披露）必须被视为一级隐私风险；评估必须衡量智能体能够推断出哪些身份。传统匿名化方法面临新的挑战，需要重新考虑隐私保护策略。

Abstract: Anonymization is widely treated as a practical safeguard because re-identifying anonymous records was historically costly, requiring domain expertise, tailored algorithms, and manual corroboration. We study a growing privacy risk that may weaken this barrier: LLM-based agents can autonomously reconstruct real-world identities from scattered, individually non-identifying cues. By combining these sparse cues with public information, agents resolve identities without bespoke engineering. We formalize this threat as \emph{inference-driven linkage} and systematically evaluate it across three settings: classical linkage scenarios (Netflix and AOL), \emph{InferLink} (a controlled benchmark varying task intent, shared cues, and attacker knowledge), and modern text-rich artifacts. Without task-specific heuristics, agents successfully execute both fixed-pool matching and open-ended identity resolution. In the Netflix Prize setting, an agent reconstructs 79.2\% of identities, significantly outperforming a 56.0\% classical baseline. Furthermore, linkage emerges not only under explicit adversarial prompts but also as a byproduct of benign cross-source analysis in \emph{InferLink} and unstructured research narratives. These findings establish that identity inference -- not merely explicit information disclosure -- must be treated as a first-class privacy risk; evaluations must measure what identities an agent can infer.

</details>


### [212] [From Topic to Transition Structure: Unsupervised Concept Discovery at Corpus Scale via Predictive Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18420)
*Jason Dury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过文本内共现关系训练对比模型，从预训练嵌入中提取文本的过渡结构概念（文本做什么），而非语义内容（文本关于什么），形成多粒度概念图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统嵌入模型基于语义内容分组文本，但文本内的时序共现关系能揭示不同的结构模式——文本的过渡结构概念或功能模式。研究者希望探索这种结构如何反映文本的"功能"而非"主题"。

Method: 在373百万个共现对上训练29.4M参数的对比模型，将预训练嵌入映射到关联空间。模型在容量约束下（42.75%准确率）必须压缩重复模式而非记忆单个共现。通过k=50到2000的六个粒度进行聚类，形成多分辨率概念图谱。

Result: 关联空间聚类产生功能导向的概念：从"直接对抗"、"抒情冥想"等广泛模式到"水手方言"、"法庭交叉询问"等具体语域和场景模板。k=100时，每个聚类平均包含4,508本书（共9,766本），确认了语料库范围的模式。与嵌入相似性聚类对比显示，原始嵌入按主题分组，而关联空间聚类按功能、语域和文学传统分组。

Conclusion: 该方法将预测关联记忆从情景回忆扩展到概念形成：在压缩下的多轮次对比训练提取了可迁移到未见文本的结构模式。关联模型将每部小说集中到连贯聚类的选择性子集，而原始嵌入分配则几乎饱和所有聚类，验证了功能导向概念提取的有效性。

Abstract: Embedding models group text by semantic content, what text is about. We show that temporal co-occurrence within texts discovers a different kind of structure: recurrent transition-structure concepts or what text does. We train a 29.4M-parameter contrastive model on 373 million co-occurrence pairs from 9,766 Project Gutenberg texts (24.96 million passages), mapping pre-trained embeddings into an association space where passages with similar transition structure cluster together. Under capacity constraint (42.75% accuracy), the model must compress across recurring patterns rather than memorise individual co-occurrences. Clustering at six granularities (k=50 to k=2,000) produces a multi-resolution concept map; from broad modes like "direct confrontation" and "lyrical meditation" to precise registers and scene templates like "sailor dialect" and "courtroom cross-examination." At k=100, clusters average 4,508 books each (of 9,766), confirming corpus-wide patterns. Direct comparison with embedding-similarity clustering shows that raw embeddings group by topic while association-space clusters group by function, register, and literary tradition. Unseen novels are assigned to existing clusters without retraining; the association model concentrates each novel into a selective subset of coherent clusters, while raw embedding assignment saturates nearly all clusters. Validation controls address positional, length, and book-concentration confounds. The method extends Predictive Associative Memory (PAM, arXiv:2602.11322) from episodic recall to concept formation: where PAM recalls specific associations, multi-epoch contrastive training under compression extracts structural patterns that transfer to unseen texts, the same framework producing qualitatively different behaviour in a different regime.

</details>


### [213] [Prune-then-Quantize or Quantize-then-Prune? Understanding the Impact of Compression Order in Joint Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18426)
*Minjun Kim,Jaehyeon Choi,Hyunwoo Yang,Jongjin Kim,Jinho Song,U Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了联合模型压缩中压缩顺序的重要性，提出了渐进强度假设：较弱的扰动应先于较强的扰动，并通过理论和实验验证了这一假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合模型压缩（如剪枝和量化结合）已成为提高效率的强大策略，但压缩顺序这一核心因素被忽视。先前研究要么假设技术正交性，要么仅在高度受限情况下考察，导致压缩顺序对模型性能的影响机制不明确。

Method: 1. 形式化压缩顺序优化问题；2. 提出渐进强度假设：较弱的压缩方法应先于较强的压缩方法应用；3. 提供理论保证，表明一种顺序的相对优势随底层性能差距增加而增加；4. 在语言和视觉模型上进行广泛实验验证；5. 扩展验证到多阶段压缩和混合精度量化等更广泛设置。

Result: 理论和实验验证了渐进强度假设的正确性。实验表明，遵循"弱扰动先于强扰动"的顺序能获得更好的模型性能。该假设在语言和视觉模型中均有效，且可推广到多阶段压缩和混合精度量化等场景。

Conclusion: 压缩顺序在联合模型压缩中至关重要，渐进强度假设为确定最优压缩顺序提供了理论指导。该研究填补了联合压缩顺序研究的空白，为实际应用中的压缩流程设计提供了科学依据。

Abstract: What happens when multiple compression methods are combined-does the order in which they are applied matter? Joint model compression has emerged as a powerful strategy to achieve higher efficiency by combining multiple methods such as pruning and quantization. A central but underexplored factor in joint model compression is the compression order, or the sequence of different methods within the compression pipeline. Most prior studies have either sidestepped the issue by assuming orthogonality between techniques, while a few have examined them only in highly constrained cases. Consequently, the broader role of compression order in shaping model performance remains poorly understood. In this paper, we address the overlooked problem of compression order and provide both theoretical and empirical analysis. We formulate the problem of optimizing the compression order and introduce the Progressive Intensity Hypothesis, which states that weaker perturbations should precede stronger ones. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that the relative benefit of one order increases with the underlying performance gap. Extensive experiments on both language and vision models validate the hypothesis, and further show its generality to broader setups such as multi-stage compression and mixed-precision quantization.

</details>


### [214] [AS2 -- Attention-Based Soft Answer Sets: An End-to-End Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Reasoning Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18436)
*Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AS2是一种完全可微的神经符号架构，用软连续近似替代离散求解器，实现端到端训练，在视觉数独和MNIST加法任务上达到高精度和约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经符号AI系统在神经感知模块和离散符号求解器之间存在不可微边界，阻碍了约束满足反馈在训练期间传播到感知编码器，限制了端到端学习能力。

Method: 提出AS2架构：1) 用软连续近似替代ASP立即后果算子T_P；2) 在整个前向传播中维护有限符号域上的位置概率分布；3) 通过最小化概率提升T_P的不动点残差进行端到端训练；4) 使用约束组成员嵌入而非传统位置嵌入；5) 采用贪婪约束解码无需外部求解器。

Result: 在视觉数独任务上：99.89%单元格精度，100%约束满足（经Clingo验证）；在MNIST加法任务上（N∈{2,4,8}个加数）：所有规模下数字精度超过99.7%。

Conclusion: 软可微不动点算子结合约束感知注意力和声明式约束规范，能够匹配或超越基于流水线和求解器的神经符号系统，同时保持完全端到端可微性。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic artificial intelligence (AI) systems typically couple a neural perception module to a discrete symbolic solver through a non-differentiable boundary, preventing constraint-satisfaction feedback from reaching the perception encoder during training. We introduce AS2 (Attention-Based Soft Answer Sets), a fully differentiable neuro-symbolic architecture that replaces the discrete solver with a soft, continuous approximation of the Answer Set Programming (ASP) immediate consequence operator $T_P$. AS2 maintains per-position probability distributions over a finite symbol domain throughout the forward pass and trains end-to-end by minimizing the fixed-point residual of a probabilistic lift of $T_P$, thereby differentiating through the constraint check without invoking an external solver at either training or inference time. The architecture is entirely free of conventional positional embeddings. Instead, it encodes problem structure through constraint-group membership embeddings that directly reflect the declarative ASP specification, making the model agnostic to arbitrary position indexing. On Visual Sudoku, AS2 achieves 99.89% cell accuracy and 100% constraint satisfaction (verified by Clingo) across 1,000 test boards, using a greedy constrained decoding procedure that requires no external solver. On MNIST Addition with $N \in \{2, 4, 8\}$ addends, AS2 achieves digit accuracy above 99.7% across all scales. These results demonstrate that a soft differentiable fixpoint operator, combined with constraint-aware attention and declarative constraint specification, can match or exceed pipeline and solver-based neuro-symbolic systems while maintaining full end-to-end differentiability.

</details>


### [215] [AlignMamba-2: Enhancing Multimodal Fusion and Sentiment Analysis with Modality-Aware Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18462)
*Yan Li,Yifei Xing,Xiangyuan Lan,Xin Li,Haifeng Chen,Dongmei Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlignMamba-2：一种用于多模态融合和情感分析的高效框架，通过双重对齐策略和模态感知Mamba层解决计算效率和模态异质性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模预训练模型时代，将通用知识有效适应特定情感计算任务面临挑战，特别是在计算效率和多模态异质性方面。Transformer方法虽然擅长建模模态间依赖关系，但其二次计算复杂度限制了在长序列数据上的应用。Mamba模型作为计算高效的替代方案，但其固有的顺序扫描机制难以捕捉对跨模态对齐至关重要的全局非顺序关系。

Method: 提出AlignMamba-2框架，采用双重对齐策略：使用最优传输距离和最大均值差异来正则化模型，促进模态间的几何和统计一致性，且不增加推理时间开销。设计模态感知Mamba层，采用混合专家架构，包含模态特定和模态共享专家，在融合过程中显式处理数据异质性。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的基准测试上进行广泛实验，包括动态时间序列（CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI数据集）和静态图像相关任务（NYU-Depth V2和MVSA-Single数据集）。结果表明AlignMamba-2在多样化的模式识别任务中（从动态时间序列分析到静态图像文本分类）在有效性和效率方面均建立了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: AlignMamba-2通过创新的双重对齐策略和模态感知Mamba层，成功解决了多模态情感分析中的计算效率和模态异质性问题，在保持高效率的同时实现了卓越的性能，为多模态融合提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In the era of large-scale pre-trained models, effectively adapting general knowledge to specific affective computing tasks remains a challenge, particularly regarding computational efficiency and multimodal heterogeneity. While Transformer-based methods have excelled at modeling inter-modal dependencies, their quadratic computational complexity limits their use with long-sequence data. Mamba-based models have emerged as a computationally efficient alternative; however, their inherent sequential scanning mechanism struggles to capture the global, non-sequential relationships that are crucial for effective cross-modal alignment. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{AlignMamba-2}, an effective and efficient framework for multimodal fusion and sentiment analysis. Our approach introduces a dual alignment strategy that regularizes the model using both Optimal Transport distance and Maximum Mean Discrepancy, promoting geometric and statistical consistency between modalities without incurring any inference-time overhead. More importantly, we design a Modality-Aware Mamba layer, which employs a Mixture-of-Experts architecture with modality-specific and modality-shared experts to explicitly handle data heterogeneity during the fusion process. Extensive experiments on four challenging benchmarks, including dynamic time-series (on the CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI datasets) and static image-related tasks (on the NYU-Depth V2 and MVSA-Single datasets), demonstrate that AlignMamba-2 establishes a new state-of-the-art in both effectiveness and efficiency across diverse pattern recognition tasks, ranging from dynamic time-series analysis to static image-text classification.

</details>


### [216] [Cognitive Mismatch in Multimodal Large Language Models for Discrete Symbol Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18472)
*Yinghui Li,Jiayi Kuang,Peng Xing,Daixian Liu,Junnan Dong,Shu-Yu Guo,Yangning Li,Qingyu Zhou,Wenhao Jiang,Hai-Tao Zheng,Ying Shen,Liang Lin,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个评估多模态大语言模型处理离散符号能力的基准测试，发现模型在基础符号识别上表现不佳但在复杂推理任务中表现良好，揭示了模型依赖语言概率而非真正视觉感知的"认知不匹配"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型在解释自然场景方面取得了显著成功，但它们在处理离散符号（人类认知的基本构建块）方面的能力仍然是一个关键未解问题。与连续视觉数据不同，数学公式、化学结构和语言字符等符号需要精确、更深层次的解释。

Method: 引入一个全面的基准测试，评估顶级MLLM在五个领域（语言、文化、数学、物理和化学）中导航"离散语义空间"的能力。通过系统性的评估揭示模型在处理离散符号时的表现模式。

Result: 研究发现了一个反直觉现象：模型经常在基础符号识别上失败，却在复杂推理任务中成功，这表明它们依赖的是语言概率而非真正的视觉感知。这种"认知不匹配"暴露了当前AI能力的显著差距。

Conclusion: 这项工作强调了当前AI系统在真正感知和理解支撑科学发现和抽象思维的符号语言方面的困难，为开发更严谨、与人类对齐的智能系统提供了路线图。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in interpreting natural scenes, their ability to process discrete symbols -- the fundamental building blocks of human cognition -- remains a critical open question. Unlike continuous visual data, symbols such as mathematical formulas, chemical structures, and linguistic characters require precise, deeper interpretation. This paper introduces a comprehensive benchmark to evaluate how top-tier MLLMs navigate these "discrete semantic spaces" across five domains: language, culture, mathematics, physics, and chemistry. Our investigation uncovers a counterintuitive phenomenon: models often fail at basic symbol recognition yet succeed in complex reasoning tasks, suggesting they rely on linguistic probability rather than true visual perception. By exposing this "cognitive mismatch", we highlight a significant gap in current AI capabilities: the struggle to truly perceive and understand the symbolic languages that underpin scientific discovery and abstract thought. This work offers a roadmap for developing more rigorous, human-aligned intelligent systems.

</details>


### [217] [Cross-Domain Demo-to-Code via Neurosymbolic Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18495)
*Jooyoung Kim,Wonje Choi,Younguk Song,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeSyCR是一个神经符号反事实推理框架，用于解决视频指导机器人编程中的跨域适应问题，通过抽象视频演示为符号轨迹、识别跨域不兼容性并验证程序修订，实现任务兼容的行为适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型在视频指导机器人编程中缺乏程序理解能力，无法在演示与部署之间存在感知和物理差异时重新制定因果依赖关系，导致跨域适应困难。

Method: NeSyCR将视频演示抽象为捕获底层任务程序的符号轨迹，根据部署观察推导反事实状态以揭示跨域不兼容性，通过可验证检查探索符号状态空间，提出恢复与演示程序兼容性的程序修订。

Result: NeSyCR在任务成功率上比最强基线Statler提高了31.14%，在模拟和真实世界操作任务中均展现出稳健的跨域适应能力。

Conclusion: NeSyCR通过神经符号反事实推理实现了可验证的任务程序适应，为视频指导机器人编程中的跨域适应问题提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled video-instructed robotic programming, allowing agents to interpret video demonstrations and generate executable control code. We formulate video-instructed robotic programming as a cross-domain adaptation problem, where perceptual and physical differences between demonstration and deployment induce procedural mismatches. However, current VLMs lack the procedural understanding needed to reformulate causal dependencies and achieve task-compatible behavior under such domain shifts. We introduce NeSyCR, a neurosymbolic counterfactual reasoning framework that enables verifiable adaptation of task procedures, providing a reliable synthesis of code policies. NeSyCR abstracts video demonstrations into symbolic trajectories that capture the underlying task procedure. Given deployment observations, it derives counterfactual states that reveal cross-domain incompatibilities. By exploring the symbolic state space with verifiable checks, NeSyCR proposes procedural revisions that restore compatibility with the demonstrated procedure. NeSyCR achieves a 31.14% improvement in task success over the strongest baseline Statler, showing robust cross-domain adaptation across both simulated and real-world manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [218] [Correlation-Weighted Multi-Reward Optimization for Compositional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18528)
*Jungmyung Wi,Hyunsoo Kim,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Correlation-Weighted Multi-Reward Optimization框架，通过概念奖励间的相关性结构自适应加权优化，改善文本到图像模型的多概念组合生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型在处理多概念组合提示时存在困难，经常遗漏部分概念，导致生成结果不完整。现有奖励优化方法在联合优化多个概念时，竞争概念之间会相互干扰，限制了组合生成的质量。

Method: 提出Correlation-Weighted Multi-Reward Optimization框架：1) 将多概念提示分解为预定义的概念组（如对象、属性、关系）；2) 从专用奖励模型获取每个概念的奖励信号；3) 基于相关性估计概念难度，自适应重新加权奖励，为冲突或难以满足的概念分配更高权重；4) 在每个组内聚焦优化最具挑战性的概念，促使模型同时满足所有请求属性。

Result: 该方法应用于训练最先进的扩散模型SD3.5和FLUX.1-dev，在多个具有挑战性的多概念基准测试（包括ConceptMix、GenEval 2和T2I-CompBench）上展示了一致的改进效果。

Conclusion: 通过利用概念奖励间的相关性结构自适应加权优化，提出的框架能够平衡竞争奖励信号，强调部分满足但跨样本不一致生成的概念，有效提升了文本到图像模型的组合生成能力。

Abstract: Text-to-image models produce images that align well with natural language prompts, but compositional generation has long been a central challenge. Models often struggle to satisfy multiple concepts within a single prompt, frequently omitting some concepts and resulting in partial success. Such failures highlight the difficulty of jointly optimizing multiple concepts during reward optimization, where competing concepts can interfere with one another. To address this limitation, we propose Correlation-Weighted Multi-Reward Optimization (\ours), a framework that leverages the correlation structure among concept rewards to adaptively weight each attribute concept in optimization. By accounting for interactions among concepts, \ours balances competing reward signals and emphasizes concepts that are partially satisfied yet inconsistently generated across samples, improving compositional generation. Specifically, we decompose multi-concept prompts into pre-defined concept groups (\eg, objects, attributes, and relations) and obtain reward signals from dedicated reward models for each concept. We then adaptively reweight these rewards, assigning higher weights to conflicting or hard-to-satisfy concepts using correlation-based difficulty estimation. By focusing optimization on the most challenging concepts within each group, \ours encourages the model to consistently satisfy all requested attributes simultaneously. We apply our approach to train state-of-the-art diffusion models, SD3.5 and FLUX.1-dev, and demonstrate consistent improvements on challenging multi-concept benchmarks, including ConceptMix, GenEval 2, and T2I-CompBench.

</details>


### [219] [Reasonably reasoning AI agents can avoid game-theoretic failures in zero-shot, provably](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18563)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI智能体在重复战略互动中无需后训练即可零样本实现纳什均衡式博弈，通过合理推理能力自然达成稳定均衡行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在交互经济环境中的重复互动经常无法稳定达到战略均衡（如纳什均衡），而现有的后训练方法难以统一应用于多样化的独立开发AI模型

Method: 提出理论证明：具备合理推理能力的智能体（能够从先前观察中形成对其他智能体策略的信念，并学习对这些信念做出最佳响应）最终会在几乎所有实现博弈路径上表现出接近纳什均衡的行为；放宽共同知识收益假设，允许阶段收益未知且每个智能体仅观察自身私有随机收益

Result: 理论证明合理推理智能体能够实现路径上的纳什收敛保证；通过五个博弈场景（从重复囚徒困境到风格化重复营销推广博弈）的模拟实验验证了理论，发现AI智能体自然展现这种推理模式并内在达成稳定均衡行为

Conclusion: AI智能体无需后训练即可零样本实现纳什均衡式博弈，在许多现实战略互动中无需通用对齐程序，智能体通过内在推理能力自然达成均衡行为

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed in interactive economic environments characterized by repeated AI-AI interactions. Despite AI agents' advanced capabilities, empirical studies reveal that such interactions often fail to stably induce a strategic equilibrium, such as a Nash equilibrium. Post-training methods have been proposed to induce a strategic equilibrium; however, it remains impractical to uniformly apply an alignment method across diverse, independently developed AI models in strategic settings. In this paper, we provide theoretical and empirical evidence that off-the-shelf reasoning AI agents can achieve Nash-like play zero-shot, without explicit post-training. Specifically, we prove that `reasonably reasoning' agents, i.e., agents capable of forming beliefs about others' strategies from previous observation and learning to best respond to these beliefs, eventually behave along almost every realized play path in a way that is weakly close to a Nash equilibrium of the continuation game. In addition, we relax the common-knowledge payoff assumption by allowing stage payoffs to be unknown and by having each agent observe only its own privately realized stochastic payoffs, and we show that we can still achieve the same on-path Nash convergence guarantee. We then empirically validate the proposed theories by simulating five game scenarios, ranging from a repeated prisoner's dilemma game to stylized repeated marketing promotion games. Our findings suggest that AI agents naturally exhibit such reasoning patterns and therefore attain stable equilibrium behaviors intrinsically, obviating the need for universal alignment procedures in many real-world strategic interactions.

</details>


### [220] [CAPSUL: A Comprehensive Human Protein Benchmark for Subcellular Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18571)
*Yicheng Hu,Xinyu Lin,Shulin Li,Wenjie Wang,Fengbin Zhu,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAPSUL是一个综合的人类蛋白质亚细胞定位基准数据集，整合了3D结构表示与专家标注的精细亚细胞定位信息，用于评估基于结构的模型在该任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚细胞定位是药物靶点识别和功能注释的关键任务，虽然已知与蛋白质结构密切相关，但现有数据集缺乏全面的3D结构信息和详细的亚细胞定位标注，限制了基于结构模型的应用。

Method: 创建CAPSUL基准数据集，整合多样化的3D结构表示与专家精心标注的精细亚细胞定位信息。使用最先进的序列基和结构基模型进行评估，探索重加权和单标签分类策略，并通过高尔基体案例研究展示基于结构方法的可解释性。

Result: 展示了结构特征在该任务中的重要性，发现了决定性的定位模式α-螺旋，证明了基于结构方法在连接直观生物可解释性和推动细胞生物学数据驱动发现方面的潜力。

Conclusion: CAPSUL基准填补了现有数据集的空白，为基于结构的亚细胞定位研究提供了重要资源，展示了结构信息在提升预测性能和生物可解释性方面的价值。

Abstract: Subcellular localization is a crucial biological task for drug target identification and function annotation. Although it has been biologically realized that subcellular localization is closely associated with protein structure, no existing dataset offers comprehensive 3D structural information with detailed subcellular localization annotations, thus severely hindering the application of promising structure-based models on this task. To address this gap, we introduce a new benchmark called $\mathbf{CAPSUL}$, a $\mathbf{C}$omprehensive hum$\mathbf{A}$n $\mathbf{P}$rotein benchmark for $\mathbf{SU}$bcellular $\mathbf{L}$ocalization. It features a dataset that integrates diverse 3D structural representations with fine-grained subcellular localization annotations carefully curated by domain experts. We evaluate this benchmark using a variety of state-of-the-art sequence-based and structure-based models, showcasing the importance of involving structural features in this task. Furthermore, we explore reweighting and single-label classification strategies to facilitate future investigation on structure-based methods for this task. Lastly, we showcase the powerful interpretability of structure-based methods through a case study on the Golgi apparatus, where we discover a decisive localization pattern $α$-helix from attention mechanisms, demonstrating the potential for bridging the gap with intuitive biological interpretability and paving the way for data-driven discoveries in cell biology.

</details>


### [221] [Interplay: Training Independent Simulators for Reference-Free Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18573)
*Jerome Ramos,Feng Xia,Xi Wang,Shubham Chatterjee,Xiao Fu,Hossein A. Rahmani,Aldo Lipani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无参考模拟框架，训练两个独立LLM分别作为用户和对话推荐系统，通过实时交互生成更真实多样的对话数据，无需预先确定目标物品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练对话推荐系统需要大量对话数据，但大规模收集困难。现有模拟方法通常使用单一LLM生成整个对话，且预先知道目标物品，导致对话脚本化、不自然。

Method: 提出无参考模拟框架，训练两个独立LLM：一个作为用户，一个作为对话推荐系统。两者实时交互，推荐系统无法访问预定目标物品，但可获得偏好摘要和目标属性，通过对话真实推断用户偏好。

Result: 无参考模拟器在质量上匹配或超越现有方法，生成更真实、多样的对话，更接近真实人机交互。通过定量和人工评估证实了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 无参考模拟框架为生成高质量对话推荐数据提供了可扩展解决方案，无需将对话约束在预定义目标物品上，能产生更自然的对话交互。

Abstract: Training conversational recommender systems (CRS) requires extensive dialogue data, which is challenging to collect at scale. To address this, researchers have used simulated user-recommender conversations. Traditional simulation approaches often utilize a single large language model (LLM) that generates entire conversations with prior knowledge of the target items, leading to scripted and artificial dialogues. We propose a reference-free simulation framework that trains two independent LLMs, one as the user and one as the conversational recommender. These models interact in real-time without access to predetermined target items, but preference summaries and target attributes, enabling the recommender to genuinely infer user preferences through dialogue. This approach produces more realistic and diverse conversations that closely mirror authentic human-AI interactions. Our reference-free simulators match or exceed existing methods in quality, while offering a scalable solution for generating high-quality conversational recommendation data without constraining conversations to pre-defined target items. We conduct both quantitative and human evaluations to confirm the effectiveness of our reference-free approach.

</details>


### [222] [MedForge: Interpretable Medical Deepfake Detection via Forgery-aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18577)
*Zhihui Chen,Kai He,Qingyuan Lei,Bin Pu,Jian Zhang,Yuling Xu,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedForge是一个针对医学图像伪造检测的数据与方法解决方案，包含大规模基准数据集MedForge-90K和MedForge-Reasoner检测器，通过定位-分析推理和伪造感知的GSPO对齐，实现高精度检测和可信解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导的图像编辑器能够高保真地操纵真实医学扫描图像，植入或移除病灶，威胁临床信任和安全。现有防御方法在医疗领域不足：医学检测器多为黑盒，而基于MLLM的解释器通常是事后解释、缺乏医学专业知识，在模糊病例上可能产生幻觉证据。

Method: 提出MedForge解决方案：1) 创建MedForge-90K大规模基准数据集，包含19种病理的真实病灶编辑，通过医生检查指南和黄金编辑位置提供专家指导的推理监督；2) 开发MedForge-Reasoner检测器，采用"先定位后分析"推理策略，先预测可疑区域再生成判断；3) 通过伪造感知的GSPO（Grounding, Specificity, Precision, Objectivity）对齐，增强证据基础并减少幻觉。

Result: 实验表明MedForge在检测准确性方面达到最先进水平，并提供可信、与专家对齐的解释。

Conclusion: MedForge为医学图像伪造检测提供了有效的预检测解决方案，通过大规模专家监督数据集和定位-分析推理框架，解决了现有方法在医疗领域的局限性，实现了高精度检测和可信解释。

Abstract: Text-guided image editors can now manipulate authentic medical scans with high fidelity, enabling lesion implantation/removal that threatens clinical trust and safety. Existing defenses are inadequate for healthcare. Medical detectors are largely black-box, while MLLM-based explainers are typically post-hoc, lack medical expertise, and may hallucinate evidence on ambiguous cases. We present MedForge, a data-and-method solution for pre-hoc, evidence-grounded medical forgery detection. We introduce MedForge-90K, a large-scale benchmark of realistic lesion edits across 19 pathologies with expert-guided reasoning supervision via doctor inspection guidelines and gold edit locations. Building on it, MedForge-Reasoner performs localize-then-analyze reasoning, predicting suspicious regions before producing a verdict, and is further aligned with Forgery-aware GSPO to strengthen grounding and reduce hallucinations. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art detection accuracy and trustworthy, expert-aligned explanations.

</details>


### [223] [ZEBRAARENA: A Diagnostic Simulation Environment for Studying Reasoning-Action Coupling in Tool-Augmented LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18614)
